# Root publication requested — 30 October 2026

The user stopped further local testing and authorized committing/pushing v2,
replacing the root website, and switching between old/main and new/v2 through
branch pipelines. The original comprehensive migration gates are **not complete**.
No backend deployment is authorized by this frontend publication.

Before the stop request the current CI command had already finished successfully:
2632 frontend tests (206 files), one existing documented skip, 218 worker tests,
and 83 edge tests. The current dialog browser matrix finished with 36 passed and
28 failed. Failures include inaccessible scrolling in the earlier running build
and incorrect focus/status selectors; corrections are saved but not rerun.
The latest native checkpoint was 267 passed, before the final scroll-tabstop edit.
No new test runs were started after the stop request. Root URL fixture/assertion
changes are unrun. Root production build passed (`npm run build:production`), including index/preload and bundle budgets: 850608 bytes initial JavaScript.

Last independently recorded coverage L/S/F/B: frontend 91.61/90.19/88.68/84.44;
worker 99.66/99.72/100/98.81; edge 100/99.88/100/99.74. These predate the final
root-promotion edits and are not current release coverage. Frontend fails 98%.
Figma evidence remains bounded to the reviewed slices, not whole-product parity.
Next slice, if testing resumes: rerun corrected dialog workflows, root URLs and
release activation checks, then resume the original feature/coverage gaps.
Final commit is the commit containing this entry (see Git history); pipeline/live
activation outcome must be recorded separately after it finishes.

# Design v2 migration and regression progress

## Pending-registration dialog integration — 30 October 2026

The native confirmation has been replaced in the development worktree by a reusable accessible Angular dialog and a generation-owned controller. The controller snapshots the server-reviewed identity, rejects malformed eligibility, preserves linked-data blockers, suppresses duplicate mutations and supports Cancel/full-refresh/navigation supersession. Confirmed deletion immediately removes only that row; failed readback retains confirmed-deleted semantics and retries reads only. Uncertain deletion preserves the row and blocks further writes until read-only reconciliation or successful Administration Refresh; closing recovery explains that path. Other users’ drafts remain independent. Recovery4RED and dismissal-guidance2RED failures preceded fixes; current controller/core checkpoint329PASS. Mounted Admin/focus checkpoint290PASS; presenter now19PASS after reproducing duplicated blockers and non-focusable overflow.

Canonical family1142:36586/reference1142:36587 now has18reviewed576/358px states, adding read-only uncertain reconciliation. All variants have body/footer structure with preserved natural heights. Editable blurred overlay references1150:37668/1150:38033 and480px long-identity1150:38357/1150:38722 were reviewed; body scrolling keeps48px final actions reachable. Review entries1152:37882/1152:38265 and28prototype actions specify request-first, explicit confirmation, safe Cancel-last and read-only recovery without faking successful server decisions. Shared subtle-text token is corrected to actual Figma alias24:19→24:18/#8e9b92; existing calendar/search consumers need targeted regression. No images are used as UI implementations.

All66new native dialog candidates were manually reviewed as22full three-engine panel sheets before acceptance. Full native267/18files PASS at that checkpoint (201existing references unchanged). Real browser checks then caught a busy-opener focus race: Review is now handed off explicitly as the return target, with closing-render restoration and a surviving heading fallback. Shared/card/presenter/root53tests and focused browser case PASS. The first broader browser matrix found an actual overflowing-details keyboard-access defect; a named tab stop is now added only on overflow and responds to resizing, with existing semantic green focus ring. RED1/18PASS→GREEN19; updated browser traversal and rerun remain pending. Two other test assumptions are being corrected: fallback is the actual Administration heading, and success status needs its precise notice locator. Original failures/traces remain retained. FullCI is running under `admin-dialog-current-ci.log`; no new coverage result is claimed yet.

## Administration per-user save feedback and read-only recovery — 27 October 2026

**Recovery integration verified; full migration remains incomplete.** Acknowledged saves now retain their submitted baseline when the dependent private user read fails. The card exposes **Refresh user settings**, and Save also routes to that read-only recovery rather than repeating the mutation. Repeated read failures retain recovery; successful reads preserve later editable changes and other users’ drafts while accepting fresh server metadata. Recovery blocks duplicate Save/Refresh/Run for that user and respects route/newer-refresh ownership. Local polite feedback distinguishes saving, refreshing, saved, newer draft, write error and acknowledged-save/read error. A later edit already matching the current server value is correctly reported saved. Shared status presentation now serves audit and user feedback.

## Strict device preference reads and removed-data races — 11 October 2026

Previous goal turn made concrete progress: durable local commit acknowledgement, three-engine native persistence/rollback/retry evidence, unchanged full CI and fresh application denominators. No previous verification process remained live. Checkpoint remains **f4ac21d**; later work stays uncommitted.

`hydrateItems(..., {readFailure: 'reject'})` now separates an absent saved preference from database/request/transaction failure, waits for successful readonly transaction completion, and stages the requested entries before publishing. Successful absent primary reads may use the legacy metadata store; a failed primary read must not masquerade as absence. Strict failure is exposed for retry. Existing default callers retain tolerant read behavior. Session replacement cannot publish stale results from this hydration API, and a newer in-memory choice survives a delayed disk read. Initial API compile attempt ran no cases; meaningful **RED6 failed** records the earlier semantics. Final focused storage regressions **EXIT0 / 35 passed / 4 files**.

Additional meaningful **RED2 failed/9 passed** reproduced data resurrection after removing a preference or clearing app data while a read was pending. ### Date recovery, metadata races and canonical day interaction states (2026-10-06)

Previous goal turn was progress: date ownership fixes, actual132-case browser pass, fresh coverage and recorded evidence. This turn verified the open render-retry risk with failing tests rather than treating prior parity as proof. `stats-date-render-retry-before.log`:15 passed/two failed; filtering/rendering unavailable selected/comparison dates repeated SDK loads. Expanded null/rejection cases plus a metadata/pending case and real normalized DTO now total21 boundary tests.

Investigation/fixes:

- Automatic rendering and lightweight metadata refresh now retain failure state by request context/range/snapshot identity. Explicit selection/retry may initiate a fresh attempt. Failure records clear on actual range/route change and teardown. Existing empty/rejection recovery and unrelated snapshots are preserved.
- A metadata refresh could replace a pending loading flag and cause parallel calls. New pending-request tracking survives metadata object replacement; loading state remains true while that request is pending. `stats-date-pending-metadata-before.log`:19 passed/one failed, then the regression passed after the fix.
- Cloud DTOs normalize `snapshot_date` to local midnight. Merging that timestamp into a noon-addressed local selection removed its key and silently displayed current rankings. `stats-date-normalized-address-before.log`:20 passed/one failed. Merge now preserves the selected metadata ID/timestamp/date, so the saved ranking remains selected and is persisted under the requesting Spotify owner.

Final targeted command (same three `ng test --include` paths as previous checkpoint): **111 passed/three files, EXIT0**,21 date cases (`stats-date-recovery-final-targeted.log`). Assertions use public controller workflows, deferred SDK/storage boundaries and user-visible data outcomes; no controller-method stubs, ignores or disabled tests. The first metadata-extension test had a malformed duplicate-date fixture and swallowed a missing storage-method error; strengthening error assertions caught that (`stats-date-metadata-retry-check.log`). Its earlier19-pass invocation does not prove metadata recovery. The fixture now uses two distinct dates, verifies no swallowed errors and checks unchanged unrelated data rather than object identity.

New `e2e/design-v2-stats-date-loading.spec.ts` covers selected/comparison null and503 outcomes, stable input/filter rendering without fresh application requests, exact owner/snapshot query scope, explicit reselection recovery and axe. Network is isolated by the existing fail-closed fixture. It seeds a fully loaded default comparison so startup comparison requests are distinguished from chosen-date requests.503 exhausts the installed SDK's three bounded transport retries (four requests total), then one successful explicit recovery; Retry-After0 is exposed via the mocked response header. No retries of browser tests or screenshot updates. Initial fixture failures (wrong route, missing cache, nonexistent row selector) and later real metadata/normalized-address failures are preserved in per-run logs/trace folders. This is not an assertion relaxation: successful/recovery outcomes and exact query/count bounds remain checked.
